{\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle{x}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle{x}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle{x}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle{x}$}}}_{\lambda_{k}}^{{\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle{\eta}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle{\eta}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle{\eta}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle{\eta}$}}}_{k}}
. These accumulation points are deep-prior solutions of
{\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle{F}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle{F}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle{F}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle{F}$}}}{\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle{x}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle{x}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle{x}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle{x}$}}}={\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle{y}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle{y}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle{y}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle{y}$}}}
.

Proof.

Let
{\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle{{\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle{\eta}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle{\eta}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle{\eta}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle{\eta}$}}}}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle{{\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle{\eta}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle{\eta}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle{\eta}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle{\eta}$}}}}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle{{\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle{\eta}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle{\eta}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle{\eta}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle{\eta}$}}}}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle{{\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle{\eta}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle{\eta}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle{\eta}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle{\eta}$}}}}$}}}_{k}\rightarrow 0
,
{\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle{y}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle{y}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle{y}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle{y}$}}}_{k}={\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle{y}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle{y}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle{y}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle{y}$}}}+{\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle{{\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle{\eta}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle{\eta}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle{\eta}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle{\eta}$}}}}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle{{\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle{\eta}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle{\eta}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle{\eta}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle{\eta}$}}}}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle{{\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle{\eta}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle{\eta}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle{\eta}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle{\eta}$}}}}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle{{\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle{\eta}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle{\eta}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle{\eta}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle{\eta}$}}}}$}}}_{k}
. The regularization that satisfies (
17
) is denoted by
\lambda_{k}
and the minimizers of
\mathcal{L}(x;\lambda_{k},{\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle{y}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle{y}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle{y}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle{y}$}}}_{k})
are denoted by
{\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle{x}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle{x}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle{x}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle{x}$}}}_{k}
. According to the definition of minimizers of
\mathcal{L}
,

\frac{1}{2}\left\lVert{\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle{F}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle{F}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle{F}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle{F}$}}}{\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle{x}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle{x}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle{x}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle{x}$}}}_{k}-{\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle{y}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle{y}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle{y}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle{y}$}}}_{k}\right\rVert_{2}^{2}+\lambda_{k}\,{\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle{R}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle{R}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle{R}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle{R}$}}}({\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle{x}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle{x}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle{x}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle{x}$}}}_{k})\leq\frac{1}{2}\left\lVert{\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle{F}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle{F}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle{F}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle{F}$}}}{\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle{x}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle{x}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle{x}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle{x}$}}}^{*}-{\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle{y}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle{y}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle{y}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle{y}$}}}_{k}\right\rVert_{2}^{2}+\lambda_{k}\,{\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle{R}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle{R}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle{R}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle{R}$}}}({\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle{x}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle{x}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle{x}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle{x}$}}}^{*}).

(19)

Because
{\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle{x}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle{x}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle{x}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle{x}$}}}_{k}
satisfies
{\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle{R}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle{R}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle{R}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle{R}$}}}({\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle{x}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle{x}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle{x}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle{x}$}}}_{k})={\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle{R}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle{R}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle{R}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle{R}$}}}({\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle{x}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle{x}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle{x}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle{x}$}}}^{*})
, then

\left\lVert{\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle{F}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle{F}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle{F}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle{F}$}}}{\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle{x}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle{x}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle{x}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle{x}$}}}_{k}-{\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle{y}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle{y}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle{y}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle{y}$}}}_{k}\right\rVert_{2}^{2}\leq\left\lVert{\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle{\eta}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle{\eta}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle{\eta}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle{\eta}$}}}_{k}\right\rVert_{2}^{2}\rightarrow 0.

(20)

Then it follows that

\displaystyle\left\lVert{\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle{F}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle{F}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle{F}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle{F}$}}}\mathcal{P}_{\textrm{null}({\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle{F}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle{F}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle{F}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle{F}$}}})^{\bot}}({\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle{x}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle{x}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle{x}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle{x}$}}}_{k}-{\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle{x}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle{x}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle{x}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle{x}$}}}^{*})\right\rVert_{2}\leq\left\lVert{\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle{F}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle{F}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle{F}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle{F}$}}}({\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle{x}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle{x}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle{x}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle{x}$}}}_{k}-{\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle{x}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle{x}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle{x}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle{x}$}}}^{*})\right\rVert_{2}\leq\left\lVert{\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle{F}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle{F}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle{F}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle{F}$}}}{\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle{x}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle{x}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle{x}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle{x}$}}}_{k}-{\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle{y}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle{y}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle{y}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle{y}$}}}_{k}\right\rVert_{2}+\left\lVert{\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle{\eta}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle{\eta}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle{\eta}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle{\eta}$}}}_{k}\right\rVert_{2}\rightarrow 0.

Since
\textstyle{F}
is injective when as a mapping from
\mathcal{P}_{\textrm{null}({\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle{F}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle{F}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle{F}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle{F}$}}})^{\bot}}
to
\mathbb{R}^{D_{\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle{y}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle{y}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle{y}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle{y}$}}}}
, we can conclude that

\displaystyle\left\lVert\mathcal{P}_{\textrm{null}({\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle{F}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle{F}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle{F}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle{F}$}}})^{\bot}}({\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle{x}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle{x}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle{x}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle{x}$}}}_{k}-{\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle{x}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle{x}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle{x}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle{x}$}}}^{*})\right\rVert_{2}\rightarrow 0.

Furthermore, when
{\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle{x}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle{x}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle{x}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle{x}$}}}^{*}
is a deep-prior solution of
{\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle{F}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle{F}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle{F}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle{F}$}}}{\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle{x}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle{x}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle{x}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle{x}$}}}={\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle{y}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle{y}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle{y}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle{y}$}}}
. Due to that
\mathcal{L}({\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle{x}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle{x}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle{x}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle{x}$}}}_{k};\lambda_{k},{\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle{y}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle{y}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle{y}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle{y}$}}}_{k})
is bounded and
{\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle{R}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle{R}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle{R}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle{R}$}}}({\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle{x}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle{x}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle{x}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle{x}$}}})
is continuous, the level set
M_{t}(\lambda,{\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle{y}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle{y}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle{y}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle{y}$}}}):=\{{\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle{x}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle{x}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle{x}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle{x}$}}}:\mathcal{L}({\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle{x}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle{x}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle{x}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle{x}$}}};\lambda,{\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle{y}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle{y}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle{y}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle{y}$}}})\leq t\}
is sequentially pre-compact for any
t>0
,
\lambda>0
and
{\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle{y}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle{y}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle{y}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle{y}$}}}\in\mathbb{R}^{D_{\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle{y}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle{y}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle{y}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle{y}$}}}}
, which induces that
{\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle{x}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle{x}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle{x}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle{x}$}}}_{k}
has accumulation points. Assume
{\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle{x}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle{x}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle{x}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle{x}$}}}^{\prime}
is an accumulation point, then there is a subsequence
{\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle{x}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle{x}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle{x}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle{x}$}}}_{k(n)}
satisfying
{\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle{x}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle{x}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle{x}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle{x}$}}}_{k(n)}\rightarrow{\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle{x}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle{x}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle{x}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle{x}$}}}^{\prime}\in\mathbb{R}^{D_{{\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle{x}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle{x}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle{x}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle{x}$}}}}}
. Then
{\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle{F}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle{F}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle{F}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle{F}$}}}{\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle{x}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle{x}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle{x}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle{x}$}}}^{\prime}={\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle{y}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle{y}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle{y}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle{y}$}}}
. From the continuity of
\textstyle{R}
,

{\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle{R}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle{R}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle{R}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle{R}$}}}({\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle{x}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle{x}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle{x}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle{x}$}}}^{\prime})=\lim_{n\rightarrow\infty}{\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle{R}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle{R}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle{R}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle{R}$}}}({\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle{x}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle{x}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle{x}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle{x}$}}}_{k(n)})={\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle{R}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle{R}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle{R}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle{R}$}}}({\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle{x}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle{x}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle{x}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle{x}$}}}^{*}).

(21)

Therefore,
{\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle{x}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle{x}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle{x}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle{x}$}}}^{\prime}
is a deep-prior solution of
{\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle{F}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle{F}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle{F}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle{F}$}}}{\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle{x}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle{x}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle{x}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle{x}$}}}=y
.
∎

Then, the existence of
\lambda
that satisfies (
17
) will be discussed. First of all, the monotonicity of
\textstyle{R}
with respect to
\lambda
is studied.

Theorem 3.4
.

Let
{\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle{x}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle{x}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle{x}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle{x}$}}}_{\lambda}={\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle{x}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle{x}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle{x}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle{x}$}}}(\lambda)
be a minimizer of
\mathcal{L}({\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle{x}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle{x}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle{x}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle{x}$}}};\lambda,{\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle{y}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle{y}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle{y}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle{y}$}}})
for regularization parameter
\lambda>0
. Then the following holds:

(1)

{\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle{R}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle{R}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle{R}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle{R}$}}}({\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle{x}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle{x}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle{x}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle{x}$}}}_{\lambda})
is monotonically decreasing.
\left\lVert{\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle{F}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle{F}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle{F}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle{F}$}}}{\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle{x}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle{x}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle{x}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle{x}$}}}_{\lambda}-{\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle{y}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle{y}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle{y}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle{y}$}}}\right\rVert_{2}^{2}
and
\mathcal{L}({\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle{x}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle{x}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle{x}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle{x}$}}}_{\lambda};\lambda,{\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle{y}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle{y}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle{y}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle{y}$}}})
are monotonically increasing with respect to
\lambda
.

(2)

There exists
\overline{{\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle{x}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle{x}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle{x}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle{x}$}}}}
such that
\lim_{\lambda\rightarrow\infty}{\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle{R}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle{R}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle{R}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle{R}$}}}({\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle{x}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle{x}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle{x}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle{x}$}}}_{\lambda})={\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle{R}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle{R}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle{R}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle{R}$}}}(\overline{{\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle{x}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle{x}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle{x}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle{x}$}}}})=\min_{{\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle{x}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle{x}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle{x}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle{x}$}}}\in\mathbb{R}^{D_{\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle{x}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle{x}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle{x}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle{x}$}}}}}{\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle{R}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle{R}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle{R}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle{R}$}}}({\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle{x}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle{x}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle{x}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle{x}$}}})
.

(3)

There exists
\underline{{\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle{x}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle{x}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle{x}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle{x}$}}}}
such that
\lim_{\lambda\rightarrow 0}{\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle{R}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle{R}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle{R}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle{R}$}}}({\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle{x}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle{x}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle{x}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle{x}$}}}_{\lambda})={\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle{R}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle{R}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle{R}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle{R}$}}}(\underline{{\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle{x}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle{x}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle{x}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle{x}$}}}})
.

Proof.

(1)

For two regularization parameters
\lambda_{1}<\lambda_{2}
, the corresponding minimizers are
{\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle{x}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle{x}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle{x}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle{x}$}}}_{\lambda_{1}}
and
{\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle{x}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle{x}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle{x}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle{x}$}}}_{\lambda_{2}}
, it follows that

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2}\left\lVert{\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle{F}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle{F}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle{F}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle{F}$}}}{\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle{x}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle{x}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle{x}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle{x}$}}}_{\lambda_{1}}-{\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle{y}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle{y}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle{y}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle{y}$}}}\right\rVert_{2}^{2}+\lambda_{1}\,{\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle{R}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle{R}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle{R}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle{R}$}}}({\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle{x}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle{x}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle{x}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle{x}$}}}_{\lambda_{1}})\leq\frac{1}{2}\left\lVert{\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle{F}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle{F}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle{F}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle{F}$}}}{\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle{x}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle{x}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle{x}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle{x}$}}}_{\lambda_{2}}-{\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle{y}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle{y}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle{y}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle{y}$}}}\right\rVert_{2}^{2}+\lambda_{1}\,{\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle{R}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle{R}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle{R}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle{R}$}}}({\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle{x}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle{x}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle{x}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle{x}$}}}_{\lambda_{2}})

(22)

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2}\left\lVert{\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle{F}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle{F}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle{F}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle{F}$}}}{\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle{x}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle{x}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle{x}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle{x}$}}}_{\lambda_{2}}-{\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle{y}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle{y}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle{y}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle{y}$}}}\right\rVert_{2}^{2}+\lambda_{2}\,{\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle{R}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle{R}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle{R}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle{R}$}}}({\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle{x}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle{x}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle{x}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle{x}$}}}_{\lambda_{2}})\leq\frac{1}{2}\left\lVert{\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle{F}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle{F}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle{F}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle{F}$}}}{\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle{x}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle{x}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle{x}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle{x}$}}}_{\lambda_{1}}-{\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle{y}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle{y}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle{y}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle{y}$}}}\right\rVert_{2}^{2}+\lambda_{2}\,{\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle{R}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle{R}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle{R}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle{R}$}}}({\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle{x}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle{x}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle{x}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle{x}$}}}_{\lambda_{1}}).

Adding two inequalities in (
22
), we can obtain

(\lambda_{1}-\lambda_{2})({\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle{R}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle{R}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle{R}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle{R}$}}}({\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle{x}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle{x}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle{x}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle{x}$}}}_{\lambda_{1}})-{\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle{R}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle{R}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle{R}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle{R}$}}}({\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle{x}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle{x}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle{x}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle{x}$}}}_{\lambda_{2}}))\leq 0,

(23)

which concludes that
{\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle{R}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle{R}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle{R}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle{R}$}}}({\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle{x}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle{x}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle{x}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle{x}$}}}_{\lambda_{1}})\geq{\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle{R}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle{R}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle{R}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle{R}$}}}({\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle{x}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle{x}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle{x}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle{x}$}}}_{\lambda_{2}})
. Moreover, from the first inequality of (
22
), it is obtained that
\left\lVert{\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle{F}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle{F}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle{F}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle{F}$}}}{\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle{x}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle{x}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle{x}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle{x}$}}}_{\lambda_{1}}-{\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle{y}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle{y}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle{y}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle{y}$}}}\right\rVert_{2}^{2}\leq\left\lVert{\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle{F}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle{F}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle{F}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle{F}$}}}{\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle{x}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle{x}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle{x}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle{x}$}}}_{\lambda_{2}}-{\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle{y}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle{y}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle{y}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle{y}$}}}\right\rVert_{2}^{2}
. Due to
{\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle{R}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle{R}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle{R}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle{R}$}}}({\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle{x}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle{x}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle{x}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle{x}$}}}_{\lambda_{2}})>0
, according to the first inequality of (
22
), it is obtained that

\displaystyle\mathcal{L}({\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle{x}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle{x}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle{x}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle{x}$}}}_{\lambda_{1}};\lambda_{1},{\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle{y}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle{y}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle{y}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle{y}$}}})

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{2}\left\lVert{\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle{F}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle{F}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle{F}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle{F}$}}}{\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle{x}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle{x}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle{x}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle{x}$}}}_{\lambda_{2}}-{\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle{y}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle{y}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle{y}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle{y}$}}}\right\rVert_{2}^{2}+\lambda_{1}\,{\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle{R}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle{R}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle{R}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle{R}$}}}({\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle{x}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle{x}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle{x}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle{x}$}}}_{\lambda_{2}})

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{2}\left\lVert{\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle{F}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle{F}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle{F}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle{F}$}}}{\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle{x}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle{x}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle{x}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle{x}$}}}_{\lambda_{2}}-{\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle{y}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle{y}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle{y}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle{y}$}}}\right\rVert_{2}^{2}+\lambda_{2}\,{\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle{R}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle{R}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle{R}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle{R}$}}}({\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle{x}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle{x}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle{x}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle{x}$}}}_{\lambda_{2}})=\mathcal{L}({\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle{x}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle{x}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle{x}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle{x}$}}}_{\lambda_{2}};\lambda_{2},{\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle{y}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle{y}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle{y}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle{y}$}}}).

(2)

Because
\textstyle{R}
is coercive and positive, that is
\left\lVert{\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle{x}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle{x}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle{x}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle{x}$}}}\right\rVert_{2}\rightarrow\infty
implies
{\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle{R}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle{R}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle{R}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle{R}$}}}({\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle{x}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle{x}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle{x}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle{x}$}}})\rightarrow\infty
, there exists
c
such that
c:=\text{inf }_{{\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle{x}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle{x}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle{x}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle{x}$}}}\in\ell^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{D_{\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle{x}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle{x}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle{x}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle{x}$}}}})}\{{\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle{R}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle{R}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle{R}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle{R}$}}}({\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle{x}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle{x}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle{x}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle{x}$}}})\}
. Then, from the continuity of
\textstyle{R}
, there exists
\overline{{\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle{x}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle{x}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle{x}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle{x}$}}}}
such that
{\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle{R}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle{R}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle{R}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle{R}$}}}(\overline{{\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle{x}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle{x}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle{x}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle{x}$}}}})=\inf_{{\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle{x}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle{x}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle{x}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle{x}$}}}\in\ell^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{D_{\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle{x}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle{x}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle{x}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle{x}$}}}})}\inf{\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle{R}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle{R}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle{R}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle{R}$}}}({\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle{x}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle{x}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle{x}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle{x}$}}})
. For any
\lambda>0
, from the definition of the minimizer, it follows that

0\leq\frac{1}{2}\left\lVert{\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle{F}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle{F}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle{F}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle{F}$}}}{\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle{x}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle{x}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle{x}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle{x}$}}}_{\lambda}-{\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle{y}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle{y}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle{y}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle{y}$}}}\right\rVert_{2}^{2}+\lambda{\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle{R}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle{R}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle{R}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle{R}$}}}({\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle{x}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle{x}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle{x}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle{x}$}}}_{\lambda})\leq\frac{1}{2}\left\lVert{\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle{F}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle{F}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle{F}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle{F}$}}}\overline{{\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle{x}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle{x}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle{x}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle{x}$}}}}-{\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle{y}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle{y}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle{y}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle{y}$}}}\right\rVert_{2}^{2}+\lambda{\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle{R}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle{R}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle{R}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle{R}$}}}(\overline{{\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle{x}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle{x}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle{x}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle{x}$}}}}).

(24)

Dividing (
24
) by
\lambda
, it is obtained that

\displaystyle{\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle{R}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle{R}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle{R}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle{R}$}}}({\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle{x}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle{x}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle{x}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle{x}$}}}_{\lambda})\leq\frac{1}{2\lambda}\left\lVert{\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle{F}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle{F}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle{F}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle{F}$}}}\overline{{\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle{x}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle{x}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle{x}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle{x}$}}}}-{\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle{y}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle{y}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle{y}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle{y}$}}}\right\rVert_{2}^{2}+{\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle{R}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle{R}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle{R}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle{R}$}}}(\overline{{\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle{x}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle{x}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle{x}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle{x}$}}}}).

When
\lambda\rightarrow\infty
, it is obtained that

\displaystyle\liminf_{\lambda\rightarrow\infty}{\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle{R}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle{R}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle{R}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle{R}$}}}({\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle{x}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle{x}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle{x}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle{x}$}}}_{\lambda})\leq\limsup_{\lambda\rightarrow\infty}{\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle{R}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle{R}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle{R}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle{R}$}}}({\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle{x}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle{x}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle{x}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle{x}$}}}_{\lambda})\leq\lim_{\lambda\rightarrow\infty}\frac{1}{2\lambda}\left\lVert{\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle{F}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle{F}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle{F}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle{F}$}}}\overline{{\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle{x}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle{x}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle{x}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle{x}$}}}}-{\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle{y}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle{y}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle{y}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle{y}$}}}\right\rVert_{2}^{2}+{\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle{R}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle{R}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle{R}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle{R}$}}}(\overline{{\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle{x}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle{x}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle{x}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle{x}$}}}})={\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle{R}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle{R}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle{R}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle{R}$}}}(\overline{{\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle{x}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle{x}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle{x}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle{x}$}}}}).

From the definition of
\overline{{\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle{x}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle{x}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle{x}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle{x}$}}}}
,
{\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle{R}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle{R}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle{R}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle{R}$}}}(\overline{{\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle{x}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle{x}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle{x}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle{x}$}}}})\leq\liminf_{\lambda\rightarrow\infty}{\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle{R}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle{R}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle{R}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle{R}$}}}({\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle{x}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle{x}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle{x}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle{x}$}}}_{\lambda})
. Therefore,

\displaystyle\lim_{\lambda\rightarrow\infty}{\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle{R}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle{R}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle{R}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle{R}$}}}({\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle{x}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle{x}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle{x}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle{x}$}}}_{\lambda})={\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle{R}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle{R}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle{R}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle{R}$}}}(\overline{{\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle{x}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle{x}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle{x}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle{x}$}}}}).

(3)

Let
{\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle{x}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle{x}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle{x}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle{x}$}}}^{\prime}
be the one with minimal
{\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle{R}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle{R}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle{R}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle{R}$}}}({\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle{x}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle{x}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle{x}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle{x}$}}})
that minimizes
\left\lVert{\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle{F}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle{F}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle{F}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle{F}$}}}{\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle{x}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle{x}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle{x}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle{x}$}}}-{\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle{y}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle{y}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle{y}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle{y}$}}}_{\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle{\eta}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle{\eta}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle{\eta}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle{\eta}$}}}\right\rVert_{2}
, that is
{\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle{R}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle{R}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle{R}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle{R}$}}}({\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle{x}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle{x}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle{x}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle{x}$}}}^{\prime})=\inf\{{\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle{R}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle{R}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle{R}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle{R}$}}}({\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle{x}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle{x}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle{x}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle{x}$}}}):\left\lVert{\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle{F}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle{F}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle{F}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle{F}$}}}{\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle{x}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle{x}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle{x}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle{x}$}}}-{\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle{y}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle{y}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle{y}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle{y}$}}}\right\rVert_{2}=\min_{\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle{x}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle{x}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle{x}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle{x}$}}}\left\lVert{\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle{F}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle{F}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle{F}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle{F}$}}}{\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle{x}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle{x}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle{x}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle{x}$}}}-{\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle{y}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle{y}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle{y}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle{y}$}}}\right\rVert_{2}\}
. Then from the definition of
\mathcal{L}({\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle{x}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle{x}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle{x}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle{x}$}}};\lambda,{\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle{y}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle{y}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle{y}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle{y}$}}})
, it follows that

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2}\left\lVert{\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle{F}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle{F}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle{F}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle{F}$}}}{\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle{x}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle{x}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle{x}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle{x}$}}}_{\lambda}-{\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle{y}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle{y}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle{y}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle{y}$}}}\right\rVert_{2}^{2}+\lambda{\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle{R}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle{R}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle{R}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle{R}$}}}({\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle{x}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle{x}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle{x}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle{x}$}}}_{\lambda})\leq\frac{1}{2}\left\lVert{\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle{F}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle{F}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle{F}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle{F}$}}}{\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle{x}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle{x}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle{x}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle{x}$}}}^{\prime}-{\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle{y}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle{y}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle{y}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle{y}$}}}\right\rVert_{2}^{2}+\lambda{\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle{R}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle{R}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle{R}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle{R}$}}}({\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle{x}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle{x}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle{x}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle{x}$}}}^{\prime}).

Because
\left\lVert{\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle{F}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle{F}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle{F}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle{F}$}}}{\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle{x}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle{x}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle{x}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle{x}$}}}_{\lambda}-{\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle{y}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle{y}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle{y}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle{y}$}}}\right\rVert_{2}^{2}\geq\left\lVert{\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle{F}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle{F}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle{F}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle{F}$}}}{\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle{x}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle{x}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle{x}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle{x}$}}}^{\prime}-{\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle{y}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle{y}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle{y}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle{y}$}}}\right\rVert_{2}^{2}
,
{\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle{R}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle{R}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle{R}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle{R}$}}}({\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle{x}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle{x}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle{x}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle{x}$}}}_{\lambda})\leq{\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle{R}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle{R}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle{R}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle{R}$}}}({\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle{x}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle{x}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle{x}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle{x}$}}}^{\prime})
. Then when
\lambda\rightarrow 0
, it follows that

\displaystyle\lim_{\lambda\rightarrow 0}\left\lVert{\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle{F}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle{F}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle{F}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle{F}$}}}{\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle{x}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle{x}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle{x}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle{x}$}}}_{\lambda}-{\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle{y}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle{y}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle{y}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle{y}$}}}\right\rVert_{2}^{2}\leq\left\lVert{\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle{F}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle{F}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle{F}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle{F}$}}}{\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle{x}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle{x}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle{x}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle{x}$}}}^{\prime}-{\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle{y}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle{y}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle{y}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle{y}$}}}\right\rVert_{2}^{2}+\lim_{\lambda\rightarrow 0}2\lambda({\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle{R}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle{R}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle{R}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle{R}$}}}({\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle{x}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle{x}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle{x}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle{x}$}}}^{\prime})-{\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle{R}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle{R}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle{R}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle{R}$}}}({\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle{x}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle{x}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle{x}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle{x}$}}}_{\lambda}))=\left\lVert{\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle{F}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle{F}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle{F}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle{F}$}}}{\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle{x}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle{x}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle{x}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle{x}$}}}^{\prime}-{\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle{y}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle{y}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle{y}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle{y}$}}}\right\rVert_{2}^{2}.

Therefore,
\lim_{\lambda\rightarrow 0}\left\lVert{\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle{F}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle{F}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle{F}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle{F}$}}}{\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle{x}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle{x}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle{x}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle{x}$}}}_{\lambda}-{\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle{y}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle{y}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle{y}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle{y}$}}}\right\rVert_{2}^{2}=\left\lVert{\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle{F}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle{F}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle{F}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle{F}$}}}{\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle{x}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle{x}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle{x}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle{x}$}}}^{\prime}-{\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle{y}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle{y}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle{y}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle{y}$}}}\right\rVert_{2}^{2}
. For the decreasing sequence
\lambda_{k}\rightarrow 0\ (k\rightarrow\infty)
,
\left\lVert{\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle{F}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle{F}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle{F}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle{F}$}}}{\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle{x}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle{x}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle{x}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle{x}$}}}_{\lambda_{k}}-{\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle{y}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle{y}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle{y}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle{y}$}}}\right\rVert_{2}^{2}
is decreasing and
{\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle{R}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle{R}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle{R}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle{R}$}}}({\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle{x}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle{x}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle{x}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle{x}$}}}_{\lambda_{k}})
is increasing. And it follows that
{\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle{R}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle{R}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle{R}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle{R}$}}}({\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle{x}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle{x}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle{x}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle{x}$}}}_{\lambda_{k}})\leq{\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle{R}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle{R}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle{R}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle{R}$}}}({\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle{x}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle{x}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle{x}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle{x}$}}}^{\prime}):=M<\infty
. From the continuity of
\textstyle{R}
, the level set
\{{\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle{x}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle{x}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle{x}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle{x}$}}}:{\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle{R}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle{R}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle{R}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle{R}$}}}({\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle{x}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle{x}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle{x}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle{x}$}}})\leq M\}
is pre-compact, then there exists a convergent subsequence
{\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle{x}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle{x}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle{x}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle{x}$}}}_{\lambda_{k^{\prime}}}
. Let
\underline{{\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle{x}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle{x}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle{x}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle{x}$}}}}=\lim_{k^{\prime}\rightarrow\infty}{\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle{x}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle{x}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle{x}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle{x}$}}}_{\lambda_{k^{\prime}}}
. It follows that

\displaystyle{\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle{R}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle{R}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle{R}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle{R}$}}}(\underline{{\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle{x}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle{x}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle{x}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle{x}$}}}})=\lim_{k^{\prime}\rightarrow\infty}{\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle{R}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle{R}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle{R}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle{R}$}}}({\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle{x}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle{x}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle{x}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle{x}$}}}_{\lambda_{k^{\prime}}})\leq{\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle{R}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle{R}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle{R}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle{R}$}}}({\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle{x}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle{x}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle{x}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle{x}$}}}^{\prime}).

From the definition of
{\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle{x}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle{x}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle{x}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle{x}$}}}^{\prime}
,
{\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle{R}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle{R}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle{R}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle{R}$}}}(\underline{{\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle{x}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle{x}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle{x}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle{x}$}}}})\geq{\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle{R}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle{R}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle{R}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle{R}$}}}({\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle{x}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle{x}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle{x}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle{x}$}}}^{\prime})
. Therefore,
{\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle{R}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle{R}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle{R}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle{R}$}}}(\underline{{\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle{x}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle{x}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle{x}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle{x}$}}}})={\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle{R}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle{R}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle{R}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle{R}$}}}({\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle{x}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle{x}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle{x}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle{x}$}}}^{\prime})
. By
{\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle{R}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle{R}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle{R}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle{R}$}}}({\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle{x}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle{x}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle{x}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle{x}$}}}_{\lambda_{k}})
is increasing, it is obtained that
\lim_{k\rightarrow\infty}{\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle{R}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle{R}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle{R}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle{R}$}}}({\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle{x}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle{x}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle{x}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle{x}$}}}_{\lambda_{k}})=\lim_{k^{\prime}\rightarrow\infty}{\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle{R}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle{R}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle{R}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle{R}$}}}({\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle{x}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle{x}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle{x}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle{x}$}}}_{\lambda_{k^{\prime}}})={\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle{R}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle{R}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle{R}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle{R}$}}}(\underline{{\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle{x}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle{x}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle{x}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle{x}$}}}})={\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle{R}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle{R}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle{R}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle{R}$}}}({\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle{x}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle{x}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle{x}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle{x}$}}}^{\prime})
. Therefore, it is concluded that
\lim_{\lambda\rightarrow 0}{\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle{R}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle{R}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle{R}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle{R}$}}}({\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle{x}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle{x}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle{x}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle{x}$}}}_{\lambda})={\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle{R}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle{R}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle{R}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle{R}$}}}(\underline{{\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle{x}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle{x}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle{x}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle{x}$}}}})
.

∎

Theorem
3.4
prove the boundedness of
{\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle{R}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle{R}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle{R}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle{R}$}}}({\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle{x}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle{x}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle{x}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle{x}$}}}_{\lambda})
for the minimizer of
\mathcal{L}({\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle{x}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle{x}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle{x}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle{x}$}}};\lambda,\cdot)
. To further study the range and the relationship of
{\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle{R}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle{R}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle{R}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle{R}$}}}({\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle{x}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle{x}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle{x}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle{x}$}}}^{*})
and
{\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle{R}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle{R}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle{R}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle{R}$}}}({\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle{x}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle{x}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle{x}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle{x}$}}}_{\lambda}^{\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle{\eta}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle{\eta}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle{\eta}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle{\eta}$}}})
, we make the following assumption of the target image
{\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle{x}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle{x}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle{x}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle{x}$}}}^{*}
, noise
\textstyle{\eta}
and regularization
\textstyle{R}
.

Assumption 1
.

Noise
{\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle{\eta}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle{\eta}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle{\eta}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle{\eta}$}}}\in\mathbb{R}^{D_{\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle{y}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle{y}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle{y}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle{y}$}}}}
and the target image
{\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle{x}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle{x}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle{x}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle{x}$}}}^{*}\in\ell^{\infty}(\mathbb{R}^{D_{\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle{x}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle{x}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle{x}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle{x}$}}}})
satisfy:

(1)

If
\textstyle{\eta}
is nonzero,
{\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle{\eta}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle{\eta}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle{\eta}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle{\eta}$}}}\notin\text{Ran }({\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle{F}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle{F}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle{F}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle{F}$}}})^{\bot}
.

(2)

min_{{\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle{x}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle{x}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle{x}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle{x}$}}}\in\mathbb{R}^{D_{\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle{x}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle{x}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle{x}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle{x}$}}}}}{\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle{R}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle{R}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle{R}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle{R}$}}}({\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle{x}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle{x}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle{x}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle{x}$}}})>0
.

(3)

For target image
{\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle{x}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle{x}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle{x}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle{x}$}}}^{*}
and noise
\textstyle{\eta}
that satisfy (1) and (2), if
{\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle{x}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle{x}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle{x}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle{x}$}}}_{\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle{\eta}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle{\eta}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle{\eta}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle{\eta}$}}}
is a minimizer of
\left\lVert{\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle{F}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle{F}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle{F}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle{F}$}}}{\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle{x}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle{x}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle{x}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle{x}$}}}-{\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle{y}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle{y}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle{y}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle{y}$}}}_{{\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle{\eta}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle{\eta}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle{\eta}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle{\eta}$}}}}\right\rVert_{2}^{2}
,
{\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle{R}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle{R}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle{R}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle{R}$}}}({\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle{x}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle{x}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle{x}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle{x}$}}}_{{\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle{\eta}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle{\eta}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle{\eta}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle{\eta}$}}}})\geq{\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle{R}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle{R}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle{R}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle{R}$}}}({\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle{x}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle{x}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle{x}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle{x}$}}}^{*})
holds.

Actually, (1) of Assumption
1
implies that for non-zero
\textstyle{\eta}
there exists nonzero
\textstyle{x}
such that
{\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle{F}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle{F}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle{F}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle{F}$}}}{\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle{x}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle{x}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle{x}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle{x}$}}}=\mathcal{P}_{\text{Ran}({\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle{F}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle{F}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle{F}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle{F}$}}})}{\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle{\eta}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle{\eta}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle{\eta}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle{\eta}$}}}
, where
\mathcal{P}_{\text{Ran}({\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle{F}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle{F}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle{F}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle{F}$}}})}
denotes the projection to the range of
\textstyle{F}
,
\text{Ran}({\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle{F}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle{F}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle{F}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle{F}$}}})
. In fact, from the properties of the solution of wave equation (
1
), there exists
T_{0}>0
such that
p({\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle{r}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle{r}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle{r}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle{r}$}}},t)=0
for
0<t<T_{0}
and
p({\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle{r}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle{r}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle{r}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle{r}$}}},t)\neq 0
for
t>T_{0}
, which means that the ultrasound signal starts to be detected at
T_{0}
. If
{\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle{\eta}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle{\eta}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle{\eta}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle{\eta}$}}}\neq{\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle{0}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle{0}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle{0}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle{0}$}}}
and
{\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle{\eta}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle{\eta}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle{\eta}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle{\eta}$}}}\in\text{Ran}({\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle{F}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle{F}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle{F}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle{F}$}}})^{\bot}
, then all nonzero elements of
\textstyle{\eta}
corresponds to
t<T_{0}
, which is not worth studying. Normally, noise
\textstyle{\eta}
is assumed isotropic Normal distribution. Therefore, (1) of Assumption
1
is reasonable.

As for (2) of Assumption
1
, from the definition of
\textstyle{R}
,
{\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle{R}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle{R}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle{R}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle{R}$}}}({\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle{x}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle{x}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle{x}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle{x}$}}})\geq 0
holds for any
{\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle{x}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle{x}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle{x}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle{x}$}}}\in\mathbb{R}^{D_{\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle{x}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle{x}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle{x}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle{x}$}}}}
. Due to the randomness of
\textstyle{x}
,
\pi_{\theta_{\star}}({\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle{x}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle{x}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle{x}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle{x}$}}})<1
, then
{\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle{R}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle{R}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle{R}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle{R}$}}}({\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle{x}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle{x}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle{x}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle{x}$}}})>0
holds.

Under (1) and (2) of Assumption
1
, (3) of Assumption
1
implies that the distribution of the noisy images reconstructed from the noisy observation without regularization, that is the minimizer of
\left\lVert{\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle{F}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle{F}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle{F}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle{F}$}}}{\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle{x}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle{x}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle{x}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle{x}$}}}-{\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle{y}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle{y}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle{y}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle{y}$}}}_{{\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle{\eta}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle{\eta}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle{\eta}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle{\eta}$}}}}\right\rVert_{2}^{2}
, is far away from the distribution of the target images. Otherwise, it means that noisy and noise-free data are indistinguishable, which is not our concern.

Corollary 3.5
.

Let
{\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle{x}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle{x}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle{x}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle{x}$}}}_{\lambda}^{\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle{\eta}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle{\eta}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle{\eta}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle{\eta}$}}}
denote minimizers of
\mathcal{L}({\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle{x}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle{x}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle{x}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle{x}$}}};\lambda,{\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle{y}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle{y}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle{y}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle{y}$}}}_{\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle{\eta}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle{\eta}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle{\eta}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle{\eta}$}}})
, in which
{\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle{y}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle{y}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle{y}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle{y}$}}}_{\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle{\eta}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle{\eta}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle{\eta}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle{\eta}$}}}={\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle{F}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle{F}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle{F}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle{F}$}}}{\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle{x}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle{x}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle{x}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle{x}$}}}^{*}+{\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle{\eta}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle{\eta}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle{\eta}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle{\eta}$}}}
is the noisy observation. If Assumption
1
holds, there exists
\underline{\lambda}\geq\overline{\lambda}
such that there exist
{\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle{x}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle{x}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle{x}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle{x}$}}}_{\underline{\lambda}}^{\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle{\eta}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle{\eta}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle{\eta}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle{\eta}$}}}\in\argmin_{\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle{x}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle{x}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle{x}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle{x}$}}}\mathcal{L}({\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle{x}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle{x}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle{x}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle{x}$}}};\underline{\lambda},{\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle{y}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle{y}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle{y}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle{y}$}}})
and
{\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle{x}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle{x}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle{x}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle{x}$}}}_{\overline{\lambda}}^{\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle{\eta}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle{\eta}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle{\eta}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle{\eta}$}}}\in\argmin_{\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle{x}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle{x}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle{x}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle{x}$}}}\mathcal{L}({\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle{x}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle{x}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle{x}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle{x}$}}};\overline{\lambda},{\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle{y}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle{y}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle{y}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle{y}$}}})
satisfy

{\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle{R}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle{R}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle{R}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle{R}$}}}({\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle{x}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle{x}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle{x}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle{x}$}}}_{\underline{\lambda}}^{\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle{\eta}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle{\eta}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle{\eta}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle{\eta}$}}})\leq{\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle{R}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle{R}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle{R}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle{R}$}}}({\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle{x}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle{x}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle{x}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle{x}$}}}^{*})
and
{\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle{R}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle{R}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle{R}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle{R}$}}}({\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle{x}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle{x}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle{x}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle{x}$}}}_{\overline{\lambda}}^{\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle{\eta}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle{\eta}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle{\eta}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle{\eta}$}}})\geq{\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle{R}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle{R}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle{R}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle{R}$}}}({\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle{x}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle{x}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle{x}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle{x}$}}}^{*})
.

Proof.

From Theorem
3.4
, there exists
\underline{{\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle{x}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle{x}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle{x}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle{x}$}}}}
such that

\displaystyle\lim_{\lambda\rightarrow\infty}{\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle{R}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle{R}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle{R}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle{R}$}}}({\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle{x}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle{x}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle{x}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle{x}$}}}_{\lambda}^{\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle{\eta}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle{\eta}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle{\eta}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle{\eta}$}}})={\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle{R}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle{R}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle{R}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle{R}$}}}(\underline{{\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle{x}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle{x}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle{x}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle{x}$}}}})=\min_{{\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle{x}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle{x}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle{x}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle{x}$}}}\in\mathbb{R}^{D_{\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle{x}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle{x}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle{x}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle{x}$}}}}}{\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle{R}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle{R}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle{R}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle{R}$}}}({\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle{x}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle{x}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle{x}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle{x}$}}}).

So for any
\varepsilon>0
, there is
\underline{\lambda}
such that when
\lambda>\underline{\lambda}
,
{\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle{R}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle{R}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle{R}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle{R}$}}}({\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle{x}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle{x}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle{x}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle{x}$}}}_{\lambda}^{\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle{\eta}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle{\eta}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle{\eta}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle{\eta}$}}})<{\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle{R}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle{R}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle{R}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle{R}$}}}(\underline{{\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle{x}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle{x}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle{x}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle{x}$}}}})+\varepsilon
. Then
{\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle{R}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle{R}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle{R}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle{R}$}}}({\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle{x}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle{x}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle{x}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle{x}$}}}_{\lambda}^{{\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle{\eta}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle{\eta}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle{\eta}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle{\eta}$}}}})<{\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle{R}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle{R}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle{R}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle{R}$}}}(\underline{{\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle{x}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle{x}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle{x}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle{x}$}}}})+\varepsilon\leq{\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle{R}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle{R}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle{R}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle{R}$}}}({\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle{x}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle{x}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle{x}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle{x}$}}}^{*})+\varepsilon
. Therefore,
{\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle{R}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle{R}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle{R}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle{R}$}}}({\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle{x}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle{x}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle{x}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle{x}$}}}_{\lambda}^{{\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle{\eta}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle{\eta}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle{\eta}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle{\eta}$}}}})\leq{\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle{R}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle{R}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle{R}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle{R}$}}}({\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle{x}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle{x}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle{x}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle{x}$}}}^{*})
. Similarly, there exists
\overline{{\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle{x}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle{x}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle{x}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle{x}$}}}}
such that

\displaystyle\lim_{\lambda\rightarrow 0}{\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle{R}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle{R}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle{R}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle{R}$}}}({\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle{x}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle{x}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle{x}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle{x}$}}}_{\lambda}^{\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle{\eta}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle{\eta}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle{\eta}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle{\eta}$}}})={\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle{R}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle{R}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle{R}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle{R}$}}}(\overline{{\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle{x}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle{x}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle{x}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle{x}$}}}}).

From the proof of Theorem
3.4
,
\overline{{\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle{x}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle{x}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle{x}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle{x}$}}}}
minimizes
\left\lVert{\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle{F}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle{F}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle{F}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle{F}$}}}{\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle{x}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle{x}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle{x}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle{x}$}}}^{*}-{\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle{y}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle{y}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle{y}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle{y}$}}}_{\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle{\eta}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle{\eta}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle{\eta}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle{\eta}$}}}\right\rVert_{2}
and
